### 20. tool_result

1→"""TrackNet-based hybrid play segmenter.
2→
3→Same three-phase shape as HybridYoloSegmenter, but Phase 2 (candidate
4→generation) uses a TrackNetV4 *shuttle* trajectory instead of YOLO *person*
5→motion. TrackNet runs in WSL2 (see backend/detectors/tracknet_runner.py and
6→docs/DECISIONS.md ADR-001); the shuttle trajectory is converted into candidate
7→rally windows, which are then handed to the AI provider for semantic boundary
8→confirmation (Phase 3) and written to the DB (Phase 4).
9→
10→Rationale: a tiny, fast, motion-blurred shuttle is exactly what general
11→detectors miss; a purpose-built trajectory model localises it far more
12→precisely, giving cleaner candidate rallies than person-velocity heuristics.
13→"""
14→
15→import os
16→import time
17→import logging
18→import concurrent.futures
19→from typing import List, Dict, Any
20→
21→import cv2
22→
23→from backend.pipeline.segmenters.base import BasePlaySegmenter, segmenter_registry
24→from backend.utils.video import get_video_duration, extract_video_chunk
25→from backend.sports import sport_registry
26→from backend.providers import provider_registry
27→from backend.pipeline.detectors.tracknet_runner import TrackNetRunner, TrackNetConfig
28→
29→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
30→
31→
32→def _fmt_ts(seconds: float) -> str:
33→    seconds = max(0, int(seconds))
34→    h, rem = divmod(seconds, 3600)
35→    m, s = divmod(rem, 60)
36→    return f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}"
37→
38→
39→class TrackNetHybridSegmenter(BasePlaySegmenter):
40→    @property
41→    def name(self) -> str:
42→        return "tracknet_hybrid"
43→
44→    @property
45→    def description(self) -> str:
46→        return ("Two-stage pipeline: TrackNetV4 shuttle-trajectory detection (WSL2) for precise "
47→                "candidate rallies + AI provider for high-precision semantic boundaries.")
48→
49→    @staticmethod
50→    def _probe_fps_width(video_path: str) -> tuple:
51→        """Return (fps, frame_width) for a video, with sensible fallbacks."""
52→        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
53→        fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS) if cap.isOpened() else 0.0
54→        width = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH) if cap.isOpened() else 0.0
55→        cap.release()
56→        return (fps if fps > 0 else 30.0, width if width > 0 else 1280.0)
57→
58→    def process_video(self, video_id: str, video_path: str, player_pool: List[str] = None,
59→                      max_frames: int = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
60→        # --- Sport profile + AI provider wiring (identical to the other segmenters) ---
61→        sport_name = self.config.get("sport", "badminton")
62→        try:
63→            sport_profile = sport_registry.get(sport_name)
64→        except KeyError as e:
65→            logger.error(str(e))
66→            return []
67→
68→        idx_cfg = self.config.get("indexing", {})
69→        provider_name = self.config.get("ai_provider", idx_cfg.get("ai_provider", "gemini"))
70→        model_name = self.config.get("ai_model", idx_cfg.get("ai_model", "gemini-2.5-flash"))
71→
72→        api_key_env_var = f"{provider_name.upper()}_API_KEY"
73→        api_key = os.environ.get(api_key_env_var)
74→        if not api_key:
75→            logger.error(
76→                f"{api_key_env_var} environment variable is not set! "
77→                f"To run the {provider_name} handoff, set your API key. "
78→                f"In PowerShell: $env:{api_key_env_var}=\"your_api_key_here\""
79→            )
80→            return []
81→        try:
82→            provider = provider_registry.get(provider_name, api_key=api_key, model_name=model_name)
83→        except KeyError as e:
84→            logger.error(str(e))
85→            return []
86→
87→        video_duration = get_video_duration(video_path)
88→        if video_duration <= 0:
89→            logger.error("Invalid video duration.")
90→            return []
91→        fps, frame_width = self._probe_fps_width(video_path)
92→
93→        # --- TrackNet runner config + thresholds ---
94→        tn_cfg = TrackNetConfig.from_indexing_cfg(idx_cfg)
95→        runner = TrackNetRunner(tn_cfg)
96→
97→        tn = idx_cfg.get("tracknet", {})
98→        velocity_thresh = tn.get("velocity_threshold", 0.02)   # normalised shuttle displacement/sec
99→        merge_gap = idx_cfg.get("dead_time_merge_gap", 2.0)
100→        min_window_duration = idx_cfg.get("min_action_window_duration", 2.0)
101→        handoff_padding = idx_cfg.get("ai_handoff_padding", 2.0)
102→        ai_max_workers = idx_cfg.get("ai_max_workers", 5)
103→        log_candidates = idx_cfg.get("log_yolo_candidates", True)
104→        store_candidates = idx_cfg.get("store_yolo_candidates", True)
105→
106→        detection_params = {
107→            "detector": "tracknet_hybrid",
108→            "velocity_thresh": velocity_thresh,
109→            "merge_gap": merge_gap,
110→            "min_action_window_duration": min_window_duration,
111→            "weights_path": tn_cfg.weights_path,
112→            "queue_length": tn_cfg.queue_length,
113→        }
114→
115→        # --- Phase 1: env healthcheck (fail fast with a clear message) ---
116→        ok, msg = runner.healthcheck()
117→        if not ok:
118→            logger.error("TrackNet WSL environment not ready: %s", msg)
119→            return []
120→        logger.info("TrackNet WSL env OK: %s", msg)
121→
122→        # --- Phase 2: shuttle trajectory -> candidate rally windows ---
123→        # TrackNet processes the whole video in one pass (it carries its own
124→        # frame buffering), so unlike the YOLO segmenter we don't pre-chunk here.
125→        t_start = time.time()
126→        tn_out_dir = os.path.join("output", "tracknet")
127→        csv_path = runner.run_predict(video_path, tn_out_dir)
128→        if not csv_path:
129→            logger.error("TrackNet produced no trajectory; aborting.")
130→            return []
131→
132→        points = runner.parse_trajectory_csv(csv_path)
133→        logger.info("Parsed %d trajectory points (%d visible).",
134→                    len(points), sum(1 for p in points if p.visible))
135→
136→        all_candidate_windows = runner.trajectory_to_action_windows(
137→            points, fps=fps, frame_width=frame_width, chunk_start=0.0,
138→            velocity_thresh=velocity_thresh, merge_gap=merge_gap,
139→            min_window_duration=min_window_duration, chunk_index=0,
140→        )
141→        logger.info("Phase 2 (TrackNet) completed in %.1fs. Candidate windows: %d",
142→                    time.time() - t_start, len(all_candidate_windows))
143→
144→        if log_candidates:
145→            for w in all_candidate_windows:
146→                logger.info(
147→                    f"[TrackNet rally] {_fmt_ts(w['start'])}-{_fmt_ts(w['end'])} "
148→                    f"({w['end'] - w['start']:.1f}s) | frames={w['active_frame_count']} "
149→                    f"peak_v={w['peak_velocity']:.3f} mean_v={w['mean_velocity']:.3f}"
150→                )
151→
152→        # Persist raw candidates for the fine-tuning dataset (same table as YOLO).
153→        candidate_ids = [None] * len(all_candidate_windows)
154→        if store_candidates:
155→            for i, w in enumerate(all_candidate_windows):
156→                stats = {
157→                    "peak_velocity": w["peak_velocity"],
158→                    "mean_velocity": w["mean_velocity"],
159→                    "active_frame_count": w["active_frame_count"],
160→                    "track_id_count": w["track_id_count"],
161→                }
162→                confidence = min(1.0, w["peak_velocity"])
163→                candidate_ids[i] = self.db.add_candidate_segment(
164→                    video_id, detector="tracknet_hybrid",
165→                    start_time=w["start"], end_time=w["end"],
166→                    chunk_index=w["chunk_index"], confidence=confidence,
167→                    stats=stats, detection_params=detection_params,
168→                )
169→
170→        if not all_candidate_windows:
171→            return []
172→
173→        # --- Phase 3: AI provider handoff (identical pattern to HybridYoloSegmenter) ---
174→        handoff_dir = os.path.join("output", f"chunks_{provider_name}_handoff")
175→        os.makedirs(handoff_dir, exist_ok=True)
176→        logger.info("Phase 3: %s validation on %d candidate windows...",
177→                    provider_name, len(all_candidate_windows))
178→
179→        def process_candidate_window(wi: int, window: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[dict]:
180→            w_start = max(0, window["start"] - handoff_padding)
181→            w_end = min(video_duration, window["end"] + handoff_padding)
182→            w_dur = w_end - w_start
183→            w_path = os.path.join(handoff_dir, f"handoff_{video_id}_{wi}.mp4")
184→            if not extract_video_chunk(video_path, w_start, w_dur, w_path, reencode=True):
185→                return []
186→            prompt = sport_profile.get_prompt(chunk_duration=w_dur)
187→            segments, _ = provider.analyze_video(w_path, prompt, chunk_duration=w_dur,
188→                                                 label=f"Handoff {wi+1}")
189→            valid = []
190→            for seg in segments:
191→                try:
192→                    seg["start_time"] = float(seg["start_time"]) + w_start
193→                    seg["end_time"] = float(seg["end_time"]) + w_start
194→                    seg["_candidate_id"] = candidate_ids[wi]
195→                    valid.append(seg)
196→                except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as e:
197→                    logger.warning("Dropping segment with unparseable timestamps: %s — %s", seg, e)
198→            try:
199→                os.remove(w_path)
200→            except OSError:
201→                pass
202→            return valid
203→
204→        ai_segments: List[dict] = []
205→        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=ai_max_workers) as executor:
206→            futures = [executor.submit(process_candidate_window, wi, w)
207→                       for wi, w in enumerate(all_candidate_windows)]
208→            for future in futures:
209→                ai_segments.extend(future.result())
210→
211→        try:
212→            os.rmdir(handoff_dir)
213→        except OSError:
214→            pass
215→        logger.info("Phase 3 completed. AI confirmed %d semantic play segments.", len(ai_segments))
216→
217→        # --- Phase 4: DB population (identical pattern to HybridYoloSegmenter) ---
218→        segments_data = []
219→        for segment in ai_segments:
220→            try:
221→                start = float(segment["start_time"])
222→                end = float(segment["end_time"])
223→                dur = end - start
224→                shot_count = segment.get("shuttle_exchange_count")
225→                if shot_count is None:
226→                    shot_count = max(3, int(dur / 0.8))
227→                else:
228→                    try:
229→                        shot_count = int(shot_count)
230→                    except (ValueError, TypeError):
231→                        shot_count = max(3, int(dur / 0.8))
232→                metadata = {
233→                    "shot_count": shot_count,
234→                    "shot_count_confidence": segment.get("shot_count_confidence", "Unknown"),
235→                    "detector": f"tracknet-hybrid-{model_name}",
236→                }
237→                segment_id = self.db.add_play_segment(video_id, start, end, None, None, metadata)
238→                if segment_id:
239→                    candidate_id = segment.get("_candidate_id")
240→                    if candidate_id is not None:
241→                        self.db.link_candidate_to_segment(candidate_id, segment_id)
242→                    segments_data.append({
243→                        "id": segment_id, "start_time": start, "end_time": end,
244→                        "duration": dur, "metadata": metadata,
245→                    })
246→            except Exception as e:
247→                logger.warning("Skipping invalid segment struct: %s", e)
248→
249→        return segments_data
250→
251→
252→# Register the segmenter
253→segmenter_registry.register(TrackNetHybridSegmenter)
254→

### 21. tool_result

1→"""WASB-based hybrid play segmenter.
2→
3→Identical three-phase shape to TrackNetHybridSegmenter, but Phase 2 uses **WASB**
4→(nttcom/WASB-SBDT, MIT) shuttle-trajectory detection via WSL2
5→(backend/detectors/wasb_runner.py) instead of TrackNetV4. WASB ships pretrained
6→badminton weights and runs today, so this is the live shuttle-motion substrate.
7→Phase 3 (AI semantic boundaries) and Phase 4 (DB) are shared with the other hybrids.
8→
9→For the per-video *calibration* goal we mainly use Phase 2 (trajectory -> candidate
10→windows); the windowing thresholds (velocity_thresh / merge_gap / min_window_duration)
11→are what get tuned against golden rally labels.
12→"""
13→import os
14→import time
15→import logging
16→import concurrent.futures
17→from typing import List, Dict, Any
18→
19→import cv2
20→
21→from backend.pipeline.segmenters.base import BasePlaySegmenter, segmenter_registry
22→from backend.utils.video import get_video_duration, extract_video_chunk
23→from backend.sports import sport_registry
24→from backend.providers import provider_registry
25→from backend.pipeline.detectors.wasb_runner import WasbRunner, WasbConfig
26→
27→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
28→
29→
30→def _fmt_ts(seconds: float) -> str:
31→    seconds = max(0, int(seconds))
32→    h, rem = divmod(seconds, 3600)
33→    m, s = divmod(rem, 60)
34→    return f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}"
35→
36→
37→class WasbHybridSegmenter(BasePlaySegmenter):
38→    @property
39→    def name(self) -> str:
40→        return "wasb_hybrid"
41→
42→    @property
43→    def description(self) -> str:
44→        return ("Two-stage pipeline: WASB shuttle-trajectory detection (WSL2, pretrained badminton "
45→                "weights) for precise candidate rallies + AI provider for semantic boundaries.")
46→
47→    @staticmethod
48→    def _probe_fps_width(video_path: str) -> tuple:
49→        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
50→        fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS) if cap.isOpened() else 0.0
51→        width = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH) if cap.isOpened() else 0.0
52→        cap.release()
53→        return (fps if fps > 0 else 30.0, width if width > 0 else 1280.0)
54→
55→    def process_video(self, video_id: str, video_path: str, player_pool: List[str] = None,
56→                      max_frames: int = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
57→        sport_name = self.config.get("sport", "badminton")
58→        try:
59→            sport_profile = sport_registry.get(sport_name)
60→        except KeyError as e:
61→            logger.error(str(e))
62→            return []
63→
64→        idx_cfg = self.config.get("indexing", {})
65→        provider_name = self.config.get("ai_provider", idx_cfg.get("ai_provider", "gemini"))
66→        model_name = self.config.get("ai_model", idx_cfg.get("ai_model", "gemini-2.5-flash"))
67→
68→        api_key_env_var = f"{provider_name.upper()}_API_KEY"
69→        api_key = os.environ.get(api_key_env_var)
70→        if not api_key:
71→            logger.error(f"{api_key_env_var} not set; cannot run the {provider_name} handoff.")
72→            return []
73→        try:
74→            provider = provider_registry.get(provider_name, api_key=api_key, model_name=model_name)
75→        except KeyError as e:
76→            logger.error(str(e))
77→            return []
78→
79→        video_duration = get_video_duration(video_path)
80→        if video_duration <= 0:
81→            logger.error("Invalid video duration.")
82→            return []
83→        fps, frame_width = self._probe_fps_width(video_path)
84→
85→        wasb_cfg = WasbConfig.from_indexing_cfg(idx_cfg)
86→        runner = WasbRunner(wasb_cfg)
87→
88→        w = idx_cfg.get("wasb", {})
89→        velocity_thresh = w.get("velocity_threshold", 0.02)
90→        merge_gap = idx_cfg.get("dead_time_merge_gap", 2.0)
91→        min_window_duration = idx_cfg.get("min_action_window_duration", 2.0)
92→        handoff_padding = idx_cfg.get("ai_handoff_padding", 2.0)
93→        ai_max_workers = idx_cfg.get("ai_max_workers", 5)
94→        log_candidates = idx_cfg.get("log_yolo_candidates", True)
95→        store_candidates = idx_cfg.get("store_yolo_candidates", True)
96→
97→        detection_params = {
98→            "detector": "wasb_hybrid",
99→            "velocity_thresh": velocity_thresh,
100→            "merge_gap": merge_gap,
101→            "min_action_window_duration": min_window_duration,
102→            "weights_path": wasb_cfg.weights_path,
103→        }
104→
105→        ok, msg = runner.healthcheck()
106→        if not ok:
107→            logger.error("WASB WSL environment not ready: %s", msg)
108→            return []
109→        logger.info("WASB WSL env OK: %s", msg)
110→
111→        # --- Phase 2: shuttle trajectory -> candidate rally windows ---
112→        t_start = time.time()
113→        out_dir = os.path.join("output", "wasb")
114→        csv_path = runner.run_predict(video_path, out_dir)
115→        if not csv_path:
116→            logger.error("WASB produced no trajectory; aborting.")
117→            return []
118→
119→        points = runner.parse_trajectory_csv(csv_path)
120→        logger.info("Parsed %d trajectory points (%d visible).",
121→                    len(points), sum(1 for p in points if p.visible))
122→
123→        all_candidate_windows = runner.trajectory_to_action_windows(
124→            points, fps=fps, frame_width=frame_width, chunk_start=0.0,
125→            velocity_thresh=velocity_thresh, merge_gap=merge_gap,
126→            min_window_duration=min_window_duration, chunk_index=0,
127→        )
128→        logger.info("Phase 2 (WASB) completed in %.1fs. Candidate windows: %d",
129→                    time.time() - t_start, len(all_candidate_windows))
130→
131→        if log_candidates:
132→            for cw in all_candidate_windows:
133→                logger.info(f"[WASB rally] {_fmt_ts(cw['start'])}-{_fmt_ts(cw['end'])} "
134→                            f"({cw['end'] - cw['start']:.1f}s) | frames={cw['active_frame_count']} "
135→                            f"peak_v={cw['peak_velocity']:.3f} mean_v={cw['mean_velocity']:.3f}")
136→
137→        candidate_ids = [None] * len(all_candidate_windows)
138→        if store_candidates:
139→            for i, cw in enumerate(all_candidate_windows):
140→                stats = {
141→                    "peak_velocity": cw["peak_velocity"], "mean_velocity": cw["mean_velocity"],
142→                    "active_frame_count": cw["active_frame_count"], "track_id_count": cw["track_id_count"],
143→                }
144→                candidate_ids[i] = self.db.add_candidate_segment(
145→                    video_id, detector="wasb_hybrid", start_time=cw["start"], end_time=cw["end"],
146→                    chunk_index=cw["chunk_index"], confidence=min(1.0, cw["peak_velocity"]),
147→                    stats=stats, detection_params=detection_params,
148→                )
149→
150→        if not all_candidate_windows:
151→            return []
152→
153→        # --- Phase 3: AI provider handoff (shared pattern) ---
154→        handoff_dir = os.path.join("output", f"chunks_{provider_name}_handoff")
155→        os.makedirs(handoff_dir, exist_ok=True)
156→        logger.info("Phase 3: %s validation on %d candidate windows...",
157→                    provider_name, len(all_candidate_windows))
158→
159→        def process_candidate_window(wi: int, window: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[dict]:
160→            w_start = max(0, window["start"] - handoff_padding)
161→            w_end = min(video_duration, window["end"] + handoff_padding)
162→            w_dur = w_end - w_start
163→            w_path = os.path.join(handoff_dir, f"handoff_{video_id}_{wi}.mp4")
164→            if not extract_video_chunk(video_path, w_start, w_dur, w_path, reencode=True):
165→                return []
166→            prompt = sport_profile.get_prompt(chunk_duration=w_dur)
167→            segments, _ = provider.analyze_video(w_path, prompt, chunk_duration=w_dur,
168→                                                 label=f"Handoff {wi+1}")
169→            valid = []
170→            for seg in segments:
171→                try:
172→                    seg["start_time"] = float(seg["start_time"]) + w_start
173→                    seg["end_time"] = float(seg["end_time"]) + w_start
174→                    seg["_candidate_id"] = candidate_ids[wi]
175→                    valid.append(seg)
176→                except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as e:
177→                    logger.warning("Dropping segment with unparseable timestamps: %s — %s", seg, e)
178→            try:
179→                os.remove(w_path)
180→            except OSError:
181→                pass
182→            return valid
183→
184→        ai_segments: List[dict] = []
185→        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=ai_max_workers) as executor:
186→            futures = [executor.submit(process_candidate_window, wi, cw)
187→                       for wi, cw in enumerate(all_candidate_windows)]
188→            for future in futures:
189→                ai_segments.extend(future.result())
190→        try:
191→            os.rmdir(handoff_dir)
192→        except OSError:
193→            pass
194→        logger.info("Phase 3 completed. AI confirmed %d semantic play segments.", len(ai_segments))
195→
196→        # --- Phase 4: DB population (shared pattern) ---
197→        segments_data = []
198→        for segment in ai_segments:
199→            try:
200→                start = float(segment["start_time"])
201→                end = float(segment["end_time"])
202→                dur = end - start
203→                shot_count = segment.get("shuttle_exchange_count")
204→                try:
205→                    shot_count = int(shot_count)
206→                except (ValueError, TypeError):
207→                    shot_count = max(3, int(dur / 0.8))
208→                metadata = {"shot_count": shot_count,
209→                            "shot_count_confidence": segment.get("shot_count_confidence", "Unknown"),
210→                            "detector": f"wasb-hybrid-{model_name}"}
211→                segment_id = self.db.add_play_segment(video_id, start, end, None, None, metadata)
212→                if segment_id:
213→                    candidate_id = segment.get("_candidate_id")
214→                    if candidate_id is not None:
215→                        self.db.link_candidate_to_segment(candidate_id, segment_id)
216→                    segments_data.append({"id": segment_id, "start_time": start, "end_time": end,
217→                                          "duration": dur, "metadata": metadata})
218→            except Exception as e:
219→                logger.warning("Skipping invalid segment struct: %s", e)
220→
221→        return segments_data
222→
223→
224→segmenter_registry.register(WasbHybridSegmenter)
225→

1→"""Tests for the TrackNet runner's pure-Python pieces (path translation, CSV
2→parsing, trajectory -> action-window conversion). The WSL subprocess invocation
3→itself is not exercised here — these run anywhere, no WSL/TensorFlow needed."""
4→
5→import os
6→import subprocess
7→import textwrap
8→from unittest.mock import patch
9→
10→from backend.pipeline.detectors.tracknet_runner import (
11→    TrackNetRunner,
12→    TrackNetConfig,
13→    TrajectoryPoint,
14→    to_wsl_mnt_path,
15→)
16→
17→
18→def _proc(returncode=0, stdout="", stderr=""):
19→    return subprocess.CompletedProcess(args=[], returncode=returncode,
20→                                       stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr)
21→
22→
23→def test_to_wsl_mnt_path_windows():
24→    assert to_wsl_mnt_path(r"C:\Users\avidu\v.mp4") == "/mnt/c/Users/avidu/v.mp4"
25→    assert to_wsl_mnt_path(r"D:\clips\m.mp4") == "/mnt/d/clips/m.mp4"
26→
27→
28→def test_to_wsl_mnt_path_passthrough():
29→    # Already-POSIX paths are left alone.
30→    assert to_wsl_mnt_path("/home/avidullu/clips/v.mp4") == "/home/avidullu/clips/v.mp4"
31→    assert to_wsl_mnt_path("~/clips/v.mp4") == "~/clips/v.mp4"
32→
33→
34→def test_config_from_indexing_cfg():
35→    cfg = TrackNetConfig.from_indexing_cfg({"tracknet": {
36→        "wsl_distro": "Ubuntu-22.04", "conda_env": "TN", "weights_path": "/w/m.h5",
37→        "queue_length": 7, "stage_in_wsl": False,
38→    }})
39→    assert cfg.distro == "Ubuntu-22.04"
40→    assert cfg.conda_env == "TN"
41→    assert cfg.weights_path == "/w/m.h5"
42→    assert cfg.queue_length == 7
43→    assert cfg.stage_in_wsl is False
44→
45→
46→def test_config_defaults_when_absent():
47→    cfg = TrackNetConfig.from_indexing_cfg({})
48→    assert cfg.distro == "Ubuntu"
49→    assert cfg.conda_env == "TrackNetV4"
50→    assert cfg.weights_path == ""  # must be supplied by the user
51→
52→
53→def test_parse_trajectory_csv(tmp_path):
54→    csv_file = tmp_path / "v_predict.csv"
55→    csv_file.write_text(textwrap.dedent("""\
56→        Frame,Visibility,X,Y
57→        0,0,0,0
58→        1,1,100,200
59→        2,1,140,210
60→        3,0,0,0
61→    """))
62→    pts = TrackNetRunner.parse_trajectory_csv(str(csv_file))
63→    assert len(pts) == 4
64→    assert pts[0].visible is False
65→    assert pts[1].visible is True and pts[1].x == 100 and pts[1].y == 200
66→    assert pts[3].visible is False
67→
68→
69→def test_trajectory_to_action_windows_detects_moving_shuttle():
70→    # 30 fps, 1280px wide. Shuttle moves ~60px/frame while visible (frames 0..29) =>
71→    # well above the velocity threshold; then disappears. Expect one window ~1s long.
72→    fps, width = 30.0, 1280.0
73→    pts = [TrajectoryPoint(frame=i, visible=True, x=100 + 60 * i, y=300) for i in range(30)]
74→    pts += [TrajectoryPoint(frame=i, visible=False, x=0, y=0) for i in range(30, 60)]
75→
76→    windows = TrackNetRunner.trajectory_to_action_windows(
77→        pts, fps=fps, frame_width=width, velocity_thresh=0.02,
78→        merge_gap=2.0, min_window_duration=0.5, chunk_index=0,
79→    )
80→    assert len(windows) == 1
81→    w = windows[0]
82→    assert w["start"] < w["end"]
83→    assert w["active_frame_count"] > 0
84→    assert w["peak_velocity"] > 0.02
85→    assert w["chunk_index"] == 0
86→
87→
88→def test_trajectory_to_action_windows_ignores_stationary_shuttle():
89→    # Visible but barely moving (1px/frame) => below threshold => no windows.
90→    pts = [TrajectoryPoint(frame=i, visible=True, x=500 + i, y=300) for i in range(60)]
91→    windows = TrackNetRunner.trajectory_to_action_windows(
92→        pts, fps=30.0, frame_width=1280.0, velocity_thresh=0.05,
93→        merge_gap=2.0, min_window_duration=0.5,
94→    )
95→    assert windows == []
96→
97→
98→def test_trajectory_to_action_windows_splits_on_gap():
99→    # Two bursts of motion separated by a long invisible gap => two windows.
100→    burst1 = [TrajectoryPoint(frame=i, visible=True, x=100 + 60 * i, y=300) for i in range(20)]
101→    gap = [TrajectoryPoint(frame=i, visible=False, x=0, y=0) for i in range(20, 140)]
102→    burst2 = [TrajectoryPoint(frame=i, visible=True, x=100 + 60 * (i - 140), y=300)
103→              for i in range(140, 160)]
104→    windows = TrackNetRunner.trajectory_to_action_windows(
105→        burst1 + gap + burst2, fps=30.0, frame_width=1280.0,
106→        velocity_thresh=0.02, merge_gap=2.0, min_window_duration=0.3,
107→    )
108→    assert len(windows) == 2
109→    assert windows[0]["end"] < windows[1]["start"]
110→
111→

1→"""Tests for the TrackNetHybridSegmenter pipeline. The WSL/TensorFlow runner is
2→mocked out (TrackNetRunner), so these run anywhere — they exercise Phase 1-4 glue:
3→healthcheck gating, candidate persistence, AI handoff, and DB population/linking."""
4→
5→import cv2
6→import pytest
7→from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
8→
9→from backend.pipeline.segmenters.tracknet_hybrid import TrackNetHybridSegmenter
10→
11→
12→def _window(start=1.0, end=4.0):
13→    return {
14→        "start": start, "end": end, "active_frame_count": 30,
15→        "peak_velocity": 0.4, "mean_velocity": 0.2, "track_id_count": 1,
16→        "chunk_index": 0,
17→    }
18→
19→
20→def _patched(fn):
21→    """Stack the patches every pipeline test needs. fps/width probing is stubbed
22→    so cv2 internals don't need mocking (covered separately).
23→
24→    Patches are applied innermost-first, and mock.patch injects the innermost
25→    patch as the *first* positional arg — so this order matches the signature
26→    (mock_runner_cls, mock_probe, mock_dur, mock_extract, mock_provider_registry,
27→    mock_env)."""
28→    fn = patch("backend.pipeline.segmenters.tracknet_hybrid.TrackNetRunner")(fn)
29→    fn = patch.object(TrackNetHybridSegmenter, "_probe_fps_width", return_value=(30.0, 1280.0))(fn)
30→    fn = patch("backend.pipeline.segmenters.tracknet_hybrid.get_video_duration")(fn)
31→    fn = patch("backend.pipeline.segmenters.tracknet_hybrid.extract_video_chunk")(fn)
32→    fn = patch("backend.pipeline.segmenters.tracknet_hybrid.provider_registry")(fn)
33→    fn = patch("os.environ.get")(fn)
34→    return fn
35→
36→
37→def _make_runner(windows=None, csv="out.csv", healthy=True):
38→    runner = MagicMock()
39→    runner.healthcheck.return_value = (healthy, "TF 2.17 GPUs 1" if healthy else "no GPU")
40→    runner.run_predict.return_value = csv
41→    runner.parse_trajectory_csv.return_value = [MagicMock(visible=True)]
42→    runner.trajectory_to_action_windows.return_value = windows if windows is not None else [_window()]
43→    return runner
44→
45→
46→@_patched
47→def test_pipeline_happy_path(mock_runner_cls, mock_probe, mock_dur, mock_extract,
48→                             mock_provider_registry, mock_env):
49→    mock_env.return_value = "dummy_key"
50→    mock_dur.return_value = 30.0

1→"""TrackNet shuttle-trajectory runner (WSL2 subprocess adapter).
2→
3→This bridges the Windows-side indexer to a TensorFlow TrackNetV4 install living
4→inside WSL2 (ADR-001 in docs/DECISIONS.md). The flow is:
5→
6→    1. Translate the Windows video path to its WSL-visible form (and optionally
7→       stage it into the WSL filesystem to avoid slow /mnt/c per-frame reads).
8→    2. Invoke ``src/predict.py`` inside the TrackNetV4 conda env via ``wsl``.
9→    3. Read back the per-frame ``Frame,Visibility,X,Y`` CSV it writes.
10→    4. Convert that trajectory into high-motion "action windows" — the same
11→       candidate-rally shape HybridYoloSegmenter produces — so the rest of the
12→       pipeline (AI handoff, DB population) is unchanged.
13→
14→Nothing here imports TensorFlow; all model code stays in WSL.

57→class WasbRunner:
58→    def __init__(self, cfg: WasbConfig):
59→        self.cfg = cfg
60→
61→    def _wsl_bash(self, inner_cmd: str) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
62→        full = f"source {self.cfg.conda_sh} && {inner_cmd}"
63→        argv = ["wsl", "-d", self.cfg.distro, "bash", "-c", full]
64→        logger.debug("WSL exec: %s", full)
65→        return subprocess.run(argv, capture_output=True, text=True,
66→                              timeout=(self.cfg.timeout_sec or None))
67→
68→    def healthcheck(self) -> tuple:
69→        """Verify the WSL `wasb` env imports torch and sees a GPU. Returns (ok, msg)."""
70→        cmd = (f"conda activate {self.cfg.conda_env} && "
71→               f"python -c \"import torch; print('torch', torch.__version__, "
72→               f"'cuda', torch.cuda.is_available())\"")
73→        try:
74→            res = self._wsl_bash(cmd)

1→#!/usr/bin/env python
2→"""Run the FULL test suite for the badminton-highlight-indexer.
3→
4→A single, discoverable entry point so you (or a reviewer / CI) can verify the
5→whole suite before opening a PR:
6→
7→    python run_all_tests.py             # full suite, quiet
8→    python run_all_tests.py -v          # verbose (passthrough to pytest)
9→    python run_all_tests.py -k config   # filter by keyword (passthrough)
10→    python run_all_tests.py --cov=backend --cov-report=term-missing   # coverage
11→
12→Anything after the script name is passed straight through to pytest.
13→
14→Notes
15→-----
16→- The suite is fully mocked (no GPU, no network, no WSL) and runs offline.
17→- The observability **reporting** tests skip cleanly if the optional ``obsreport``
18→  package isn't installed (they use ``pytest.importorskip``). To exercise them too:
19→      pip install -e ../sports-obsreport --no-build-isolation
20→- Exit code is pytest's own, so this is CI / pre-PR friendly (0 = all green).
21→"""
22→from __future__ import annotations
23→
24→import importlib.util
25→import subprocess
26→import sys
27→
28→
29→def _installed(module: str) -> bool:
30→    try:
31→        return importlib.util.find_spec(module) is not None
32→    except (ImportError, ValueError):
33→        return False
34→
35→
36→def main() -> int:
37→    args = list(sys.argv[1:])
38→    # Default to quiet unless the caller chose a verbosity.
39→    if not any(a in ("-q", "-v", "-vv", "-vvv", "--quiet", "--verbose") for a in args):
40→        args = ["-q", *args]
41→    cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", *args]
42→
43→    obs = "available" if _installed("obsreport") else "NOT installed → reporting tests will SKIP"
44→    bar = "=" * 72
45→    print(bar)
46→    print(" Full test suite:  " + " ".join(cmd))
47→    print(f" obsreport (optional reporting dep): {obs}")
48→    print(bar, flush=True)
49→
50→    rc = subprocess.run(cmd).returncode

25→## 2. Overall Sequence (Approved Order)
26→
27→1. **Config Model (Pydantic) + Central Loader** (as a proper package) — foundational.
28→2. **Directory Structure Refactor** (high-pri, atomic follow-up PR) — move to the approved layered layout.

Logging standardization + removal of stray `print()` in hot paths. ✅ (done in feat/logging-standardization; central setup in main; replaced in motion.py, wasb_infer.py, stitcher/compiler.py, config/loader.py, main internal diagnostics; user-facing output in run_*.py and main CLI kept as print where appropriate for direct console UX. Docs updated in CODE_MAP and this plan.)
31→5. Packaging modernization (`pyproject.toml` + clean entry points).
32→6. Detector Runner Abstraction (start de-risking WSL).
33→7. Unify / extract shared logic from the two hybrid segmenters.
34→8. Input Quality UX (surface validator + audio readiness signals).

37→**Important sequencing decision (approved):** Complete the Config work **first** (this introduces `backend/config/` as a package immediately). Then perform the broader directory refactor as a **separate, atomic, high-priority PR**. 
38→
39→Rationale:
40→- Keeps the Config PR small and focused.
41→- Allows the structural change to land atomically with minimal merge conflicts.
42→- Gives Claude a clean window with no concurrent file moves.
43→- Still delivers the "level of indirection" benefit early via the `config/` package.

48→
49→Current flat layout under `backend/` (many sibling dirs + root .py files) feels unprofessional for long-term maintenance. We will trend toward clear layers without creating 15+ tiny single-file directories.

180→In parallel, **per-video observability reports** shipped across both tools (indexer PR #41/#42/#43, collector PR #11/#12) on a new shared, permissively-licensed lib **`obsreport`** (a small "report config language"). Coordination so the remaining items don't collide or duplicate:

Reframe item 8 as **extend the report** (e.g. an audio-readiness signal in `backend/reporting/` + the obsreport spec), not a new surface.
183→- **Item 7 (unify hybrid segmenters) — do this before wiring real AI-provider timing.** The report records AI timing as `not_captured`; a unified segmenter gives one place to thread the timing in. (Claude will add that hook with/after item 7.)

### 82. tool_result

1→# Video Recording Guidelines (DRAFT — thresholds pending empirical data)
2→
3→**Status:** 🚧 Draft / placeholder · **Date:** 2026-05-30
4→**Why this exists:** highlight quality is bounded by *input* quality. To get reliably good auto-generated highlights, the source video must be recorded a certain way (camera height, court position, resolution, frame rate, stability, framing). We want to (1) **derive** the minimum acceptable capture spec **empirically** from the precision/recall of the solutions we build, and (2) **publish user-facing best practices** so a user knows how to record footage that the product can index well.
5→
6→> **This is a tracked thought, not a finished standard.** The exact numeric thresholds below are **placeholders** to be replaced with values measured against our golden set. See "How we'll set the real numbers" (§3). Captured now (per the team's working agreement) so it isn't lost; to be revisited.
7→>
8→> **Update (2026-06-02):** the **frame-rate** and **camera-geometry** rows now carry **literature-backed starting values** (cited inline). They remain *starting points* to confirm empirically per §3. The golden-set **corpus** diversity spec (what footage to collect, not just how users should record) is now in [GOLDEN_SET_PHASE1_VIDEOS.md](GOLDEN_SET_PHASE1_VIDEOS.md).
9→>
10→> **Update (2026-06-07):** **AUDIO is now a first-class capture requirement** (new row below), per ADR-007 — we will use the shuttle "thwack" as a rally cue (audio+visual fusion lifts recall). This is a hard "record with audio on, mic unobstructed" ask, not a placeholder; efficacy on real GoPro audio is being validated (experiment E1 in [AUDIO_RALLY_DETECTION_PLAN.md](AUDIO_RALLY_DETECTION_PLAN.md)).
11→
12→---
13→
14→## 1. Why input capture matters (grounded in what we already know)
15→
16→ADR-004 found a concrete, capture-dependent failure mode: on **broadcast** footage the camera *tracks the players*, so player pixel-motion dips during rallies and spikes on pans/replays — making player-motion a poor rally proxy. The lesson generalizes: **the pipeline's accuracy is conditional on how the video was shot.** A **static, full-court** camera (typical of a fixed tripod recording) behaves very differently from broadcast — and is likely much friendlier to both the motion/detector stages and the shuttle-tracking substrate (WASB/TrackNet, ADR-001).
17→
18→So capture guidance isn't cosmetic — it directly moves precision/recall.
19→
20→---
21→
22→## 2. The dimensions that likely matter (hypotheses to test)
23→
24→| Dimension | Why it plausibly affects P/R | Placeholder guidance (UNVERIFIED) |
25→|---|---|---|
26→| **Resolution** | Shuttle is tiny & fast; too few pixels → detector misses it | ≥ **1080p** preferred; **720p** likely floor; 2K/4K may help shuttle tracking but ↑ compute/cost |
27→| **Frame rate** | Motion blur; boundary precision; fast-ball detection | ≥ **30 fps** badminton/pickleball; **≥ 60 fps table tennis** — 30 fps collapses fast-ball detection (**22.5% → 93.2%** at 60 fps; 120 ≈ 60, [Nature Sci Rep](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41598-023-42966-6)) |
28→| **Camera position** | Static full-court view avoids the broadcast camera-tracking trap (ADR-004) | **Fixed tripod**, elevated, behind/above one baseline, whole court in frame |
29→| **Camera height & angle** | Occlusion of players/shuttle; court keystoning | **3–5 m** elevated (~4.5 m), **~30° down-tilt**, behind one baseline, whole court + net headroom ([badminton-CV setup](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10747833/)) |
30→| **Framing / margins** | Cropping the court or players breaks detection & relevance check | Entire court + a margin; players never leave frame |
31→| **Stability** | Handheld jitter inflates motion signals | Tripod / fixed mount; no panning or zoom during play |
32→| **Lighting / exposure** | Blur & contrast affect detection and the existing blur/relevance validators | Even indoor lighting; avoid backlight/glare; minimize motion blur |
33→| **Court contrast / background** | Relevance check uses court color; busy crowds add false motion | Clear court markings; minimal moving background where possible |
34→| **Codec / container** | Pipeline already validates MP4 + decodes H.264/HEVC | MP4 (H.264/HEVC) as today; align with MONETIZATION_AUDIT.md codec notes |
35→| **Audio (REQUIRED — ADR-007)** | Shuttle "thwack" hits are a strong rally cue; audio+visual fusion lifts rally *recall* (the WASB bottleneck) — see ADR-007 / AUDIO_RALLY_DETECTION_PLAN.md | **Record with AUDIO ENABLED** (GoPro default — do not mute). Keep the **mic unobstructed**; **avoid aggressive wind/noise reduction** that flattens the sharp impact transient. Camera reasonably close to the court helps SNR (shuttle hits are quiet). |
36→
37→These map onto checks that **already exist** in `backend/validators/` (resolution, fps, blur, scene-cut, court relevance) — so several can be enforced/measured programmatically rather than only advised.
38→
39→---
40→
41→## 3. How we'll set the REAL numbers (empirical plan)
42→
43→Replace every placeholder above with a value derived from data, using the golden-set harness:
44→
45→1. Take golden-set clips (GOLDEN_SET_VENDORING.md) spanning a **range** of each dimension — e.g. the same match re-encoded at 480p/720p/1080p/4K, or clips at different camera heights.
46→2. Run the production pipeline; score with `backend/eval/metrics.py`.
47→3. Plot **precision/recall vs. each dimension** → find the knee where quality collapses.
48→4. Set the **published minimum** at (knee + safety margin); set a **"recommended"** tier above it.
49→5. Re-derive when the pipeline changes materially (new detector/segmenter shifts the knees).
50→
51→This turns "record in 1080p" from an assertion into a measured threshold — and lets us tell users *why*.
52→
53→---
54→
55→## 4. Two surfaces for the output
56→
57→1. **User-facing best-practices guide** — short, friendly, illustrated: "Put the camera here, this high, record at ≥X, keep it still." The goal is that a user can record once and get great results.
58→2. **Programmatic pre-flight check** — extend `backend/validators/` to *reject or warn* on out-of-spec uploads at ingest (resolution/fps/blur/framing), so the product fails fast with actionable feedback instead of producing poor highlights silently.
59→
60→---

### 83. tool_result

1→"""Build an observability report for one indexer run.
2→
3→Pure-ish harvest: given the validation results/context, the persisted segments,
4→and run metadata, populate an `obsreport.RunRecorder` with the raw signals. The
5→*footgun rules* (which turn signals into flags) live declaratively in spec.yaml,
6→so this module only records facts faithfully — it does not decide warnings.
7→
8→Imported only after `backend.reporting` confirms obsreport is installed, so it may
9→import obsreport freely.
10→"""
11→from __future__ import annotations
12→
13→import os
14→from pathlib import Path
15→from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
16→
17→import obsreport
18→
19→_AI_SEGMENTERS = {"yolo_hybrid", "tracknet_hybrid", "wasb_hybrid", "gemini"}
20→_SPEC_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "spec.yaml"
21→
22→
23→def load_spec() -> "obsreport.ReportSpec":
24→    return obsreport.load_spec(_SPEC_PATH)
25→
26→
27→# --- media metadata --------------------------------------------------------
28→def video_meta_from_context(val_context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]], video_path: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
29→    """Derive VideoMeta fields from the validators' shared ffprobe_meta. fps_source
30→    distinguishes a real probe from the absence of one (the fallback footgun)."""
31→    meta: Dict[str, Any] = {"fps_source": "unknown"}
32→    if video_path and os.path.exists(video_path):
33→        try:
34→            meta["size_mb"] = round(os.path.getsize(video_path) / (1024 * 1024), 1)
35→        except OSError:
36→            pass
37→
38→    ffmeta = (val_context or {}).get("ffprobe_meta") or {}
39→    streams = ffmeta.get("streams") or []
40→    fmt = ffmeta.get("format") or {}

### 84. tool_result

1→# Observability report spec for the badminton-highlight-indexer.
2→# The report ENGINE (config language, renderers) lives in the shared `obsreport`
3→# library; this file is the indexer-specific layout + footgun rules. Every
4→# actionable rule cites a README/FAQ section so a reader can self-debug.
5→name: indexer
6→title: Badminton Highlight Indexer — Video Report
7→
8→# Customer here = whoever receives the highlight reel.
9→customer_verdict:
10→  pass: "✅ Your highlights are ready."
11→  pass_with_warnings: "✅ Your highlights are ready. (A couple of minor notes above.)"
12→  fail: "⚠️ We couldn't finish generating highlights for this video. Please contact support."
13→
14→sections:
15→  - { id: run,        title: "Run",                 kind: identity,   audience: both }
16→  - { id: video,      title: "Video",               kind: video_meta, audience: both }
17→  - { id: highlights, title: "Highlights",          kind: highlights, audience: customer }
18→  - { id: cust_notes, title: "Things to know",      kind: flags,      audience: customer }
19→  - { id: stages,     title: "Processing stages",   kind: stages,     audience: technical }
20→  - { id: findings,   title: "Findings & warnings", kind: flags,      audience: technical }
21→  - { id: verdict,    title: "Verdict",             kind: verdict,    audience: both }
22→
23→rules:
24→  # The classic silent failure: 0 segments because the AI provider no-op'd on a
25→  # missing API key — NOT because the video has no rallies.
26→  - code: silent_provider_noop
27→    severity: error
28→    when:
29→      - { path: "stages.ai_handoff.details.api_key_present", op: eq, value: false }
30→      - { path: "stages.segmentation.metrics.segment_count", op: eq, value: 0 }
31→    message: >-
32→      0 segments AND no AI provider API key was set. The segmenter (see Processing
33→      stages > segmentation > segmenter_actual) hands its candidate windows to the AI
34→      provider, which silently did nothing without a key — so this is a MISSING
35→      CREDENTIAL, not 'the video has no rallies'. Fix: put GEMINI_API_KEY=<key> in a
36→      .env file in the project root and re-run (see README#Q7).
37→    faq_ref: "README#Q7"
38→    customer_message: >-
39→      We couldn't generate highlights for this video due to a configuration issue
40→      on our side. Please contact support.
41→
42→  # API key WAS present but an AI segmenter still produced nothing — likely a
43→  # provider error / quota / WSL healthcheck issue rather than a truly empty video.
44→  - code: ai_empty_vs_no_rallies
45→    severity: warn
46→    when:
47→      - { path: "stages.ai_handoff.details.ai_based", op: eq, value: true }
48→      - { path: "stages.ai_handoff.details.api_key_present", op: eq, value: true }
49→      - { path: "stages.segmentation.metrics.segment_count", op: eq, value: 0 }
50→    message: >-
51→      An AI-based segmenter returned 0 segments even though the API key is set.
52→      Empty can mean a provider error, exhausted quota, or (for WASB/TrackNet) a
53→      failed WSL healthcheck — these all return [] silently. Re-run with -u for
54→      unbuffered logs and check the provider/WSL output before concluding 'no rallies'.
55→    faq_ref: "README#Q7"
56→
57→  # fps was assumed rather than probed -> velocity thresholds + timing may be wrong.
58→  - code: fps_fallback_used
59→    severity: warn
60→    when:
61→      - { path: "video_meta.fps_source", op: eq, value: fallback }
62→    message: >-
63→      fps was NOT probed from the file — a fallback default (shown as the fps value)
64→      was used. Because rally timing and motion thresholds scale with fps, boundaries
65→      and motion sensitivity may be miscalibrated. This usually means validation didn't
66→      run (it probes the file) or ffprobe couldn't read r_frame_rate. Confirm the real
67→      fps with ffprobe and re-run.
68→    faq_ref: "README#Q14"
69→
70→  # The motion baseline fabricates its per-segment metadata/events — they are not
71→  # measured. Warn so nobody trusts them as ground truth.
72→  - code: synthetic_motion_metadata
73→    severity: warn
74→    when:
75→      - { path: "stages.segmentation.details.segmenter_actual", op: eq, value: motion }
76→    message: >-
77→      The 'motion' segmenter was used: its per-segment metadata (intensity, speed,
78→      events, server/receiver) are PLACEHOLDER values (unmeasured) — fabricated by the
79→      heuristic, NOT derived from the video — so a PASS with segments here does not mean
80→      the metadata is trustworthy. Use yolo_hybrid/gemini for real shot metadata.

66→
67→# --- stage builders --------------------------------------------------------
68→def _validation_stage(rec: "obsreport.RunRecorder", val_results: List[Any]) -> None:
69→    with rec.stage("validation") as st:
70→        total = len(val_results)
71→        passed = sum(1 for r in val_results if getattr(r, "passed", False))
72→        st.add_metric("checks_passed", passed)
73→        st.add_metric("checks_total", total)
74→        failures = [r for r in val_results if not getattr(r, "passed", True)]
75→        for r in failures:
76→            st.messages.append(f"FAILED {getattr(r, 'validator_name', '?')}: {getattr(r, 'message', '')}")
77→        st.status = "error" if failures else ("ok" if total else "not_run")
78→
79→
80→def _segmentation_stage(
81→    rec: "obsreport.RunRecorder",
82→    play_segments: List[Dict[str, Any]],
83→    candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]],
84→    segmenter_requested: Optional[str],
85→    segmenter_actual: Optional[str],
86→    config_default: Optional[str],
87→    was_reingest: bool,
88→    ran: bool,
89→) -> None:
90→    with rec.stage("segmentation") as st:
91→        if not ran:
92→            st.status = "not_run"
93→        seg_count = len(play_segments)
94→        total_play = round(sum(float(s.get("duration", 0.0)) for s in play_segments), 1)
95→        detector_tag = None
96→        if play_segments:
97→            detector_tag = (play_segments[0].get("metadata") or {}).get("detector")
98→        st.add_metric("candidate_windows", len(candidates))
99→        st.add_metric("segment_count", seg_count, audience="both")
100→        st.add_metric("total_play_s", total_play, unit="s")
101→        # "diverges" only when a segmenter actually RAN, the user did NOT explicitly
102→        # pick it, and it differs from config's default (the genuine surprise case).
103→        # An explicit --segmenter override or a halted run is not a divergence.
104→        matches_default = True
105→        if ran and segmenter_actual and config_default:
106→            matches_default = (segmenter_actual == config_default)
107→        st.details.update({
108→            "segmenter_requested": segmenter_requested,
109→            "segmenter_actual": segmenter_actual,
110→            "config_default": config_default,
111→            "segmenter_explicitly_requested": segmenter_requested is not None,
112→            "matches_config_default": matches_default,
113→            "was_reingest": was_reingest,
114→            "detector": detector_tag,
115→        })
116→        # Surface metadata trustworthiness at the data level (not just in a warning):
117→        # the motion baseline fabricates per-segment metadata; everything else measures it.
118→        if ran and segmenter_actual:
119→            st.details["metadata_source"] = (
120→                "synthetic/heuristic — NOT measured" if segmenter_actual == "motion"
121→                else "measured (detector/AI)"
122→            )
123→
124→
125→def _ai_stage(rec: "obsreport.RunRecorder", config: Dict[str, Any], segmenter_actual: Optional[str],
126→              play_segments: List[Dict[str, Any]], ran: bool) -> None:
127→    """Only meaningful for AI-based segmenters. Records whether the provider key was
128→    present — the signal the silent_provider_noop rule keys on."""
129→    ai_based = segmenter_actual in _AI_SEGMENTERS
130→    provider = (config.get("ai_provider") or "gemini")
131→    key_env = f"{provider.upper()}_API_KEY"
132→    with rec.stage("ai_handoff") as st:
133→        if not ran or not ai_based:
134→            st.status = "not_run" if not ran else "skipped"
135→        st.details.update({
136→            "ai_based": ai_based,
137→            "provider": provider if ai_based else None,
138→            "model": config.get("ai_model") if ai_based else None,
139→            "api_key_present": bool(os.environ.get(key_env)) if ai_based else None,

41→def test_every_rule_faq_ref_resolves_to_a_readme_heading():
42→    spec = load_spec(SPEC)
43→    headings = _headings(README.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
44→    unresolved = []
45→    for rule in spec.rules:
46→        if not rule.faq_ref:
47→            continue
48→        file_part, _, anchor = rule.faq_ref.partition("#")
49→        assert file_part in ("README", "README.md"), f"{rule.code}: unexpected faq file '{file_part}'"
50→        if not _anchor_resolves(anchor, headings):
51→            unresolved.append((rule.code, rule.faq_ref))
52→    assert not unresolved, f"faq_ref anchors with no matching README heading: {unresolved}"
53→
54→
55→def test_actionable_rules_have_faq_refs():
56→    """error/warn findings must cite a FAQ (info may omit). Belt-and-suspenders on top
57→    of obsreport's own spec-load validation."""
58→    spec = load_spec(SPEC)
59→    missing = [r.code for r in spec.rules if r.severity in ("error", "warn") and not r.faq_ref]
60→    assert not missing, f"actionable rules missing faq_ref: {missing}"
61→

1→"""Tests for the rally slicer's pure logic (no ffmpeg)."""
2→import pytest

4→from backend.tools.rally_slicer import (
5→    load_rally_labels, compute_clip_windows, ClipWindow, BEGIN_PADDING, END_PADDING,
6→)
7→
8→
9→def _write(tmp_path, rows, header="rally_number,start_time,end_time,start_mmss,end_mmss"):
10→    p = tmp_path / "labels.csv"
11→    p.write_text(header + "\n" + "\n".join(rows) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
12→    return str(p)
13→
14→
15→# ----------------------------------------------------------------- load_rally_labels
16→
17→def test_load_decimal_and_mmss(tmp_path):
18→    csv = _write(tmp_path, [
19→        "1,3.0,21.0,,",            # decimal
20→        "2,,,0:29.000,0:32.000",   # mm:ss fallback
21→    ])
22→    labels = load_rally_labels(csv)
23→    assert labels["1"] == pytest.approx((3.0, 21.0))
24→    assert labels["2"] == pytest.approx((29.0, 32.0))
25→
26→
27→def test_load_skips_missing_and_degenerate(tmp_path):
28→    csv = _write(tmp_path, [
29→        "7.5,106.031,,,",   # MISSED row: no end -> skipped
30→        "17,237.0,237.0,,", # zero-duration -> skipped
31→        "3,44.5,51.0,,",    # good
32→    ])
33→    labels = load_rally_labels(csv)
34→    assert "7.5" not in labels and "17" not in labels
35→    assert labels["3"] == pytest.approx((44.5, 51.0))
36→
37→
38→def test_fractional_ids_preserved(tmp_path):
39→    csv = _write(tmp_path, ["16.5,214.0,223.0,,"])
40→    assert "16.5" in load_rally_labels(csv)
41→
42→
43→# --------------------------------------------------------------- compute_clip_windows
44→
45→def test_padding_applied():
46→    w = compute_clip_windows({"1": (10.0, 20.0)}, ["1"])
47→    assert w == [ClipWindow("1", 10.0 - BEGIN_PADDING, 20.0 + END_PADDING)]
48→
49→
50→def test_clamp_to_video_bounds():
51→    # start near 0 clamps to 0; end past duration clamps to duration
52→    w = compute_clip_windows({"1": (0.2, 99.0)}, ["1"], video_duration=99.5)
53→    assert w[0].start == 0.0
54→    assert w[0].end == 99.5
55→
56→
57→def test_all_selects_every_rally_in_time_order():
58→    labels = {"2": (29.0, 32.0), "1": (3.0, 21.0)}
59→    w = compute_clip_windows(labels, ["all"])
60→    assert [x.rally for x in w] == ["1", "2"]   # ordered by start time, not dict order
61→
62→
63→def test_subset_and_unknown_skipped():
64→    labels = {"1": (3.0, 21.0), "2": (29.0, 32.0), "3": (44.5, 51.0)}
65→    w = compute_clip_windows(labels, ["3", "1", "99"])  # 99 unknown -> dropped
66→    assert [x.rally for x in w] == ["3", "1"]            # preserves requested order

[{"filename":"backend/tools/export.py","status":"added","additions":123,"changes":123,"patch":"@@ -0,0 +1,123 @@\n+\"\"\"Minimal Value Layer exports (first slice of review item 3).\n+\n+Provides timeline exports (EDL, JSON, CSV) and basic stats over the\n+play_segments returned by the indexer (or query_play_segments). These are\n+the \"time-saved editing is table stakes\" deliverables and also give a\n+machine-readable foundation for later human correction / golden feeding\n+and clip galleries.\n+\n+Kept deliberately small and pure (no DB, no side effects) so they are\n+easy to call from CLI, notebooks, or a future API endpoint.\n+\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional\n+\n+\n+def _to_list(segments: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]) -\u003e List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n+    return list(segments) if not isinstance(segments, list) else segments\n+\n+\n+def basic_stats(segments: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]) -\u003e Dict[str, Any]:\n+    \"\"\"Return simple aggregate stats for a list of play segments.\n+\n+    Keys: count, total_duration_s, avg_duration_s, min_duration_s, max_duration_s.\n+    All durations are in seconds (float).\n+    \"\"\"\n+    segs = _to_list(segments)\n+    if not segs:\n+        return {\n+            \"count\": 0,\n+            \"total_duration_s\": 0.0,\n+            \"avg_duration_s\": 0.0,\n+            \"min_duration_s\": 0.0,\n+            \"max_duration_s\": 0.0,\n+        }\n+    durs = [float(s.get(\"duration\", (s.get(\"end_time\", 0) - s.get(\"start_time\", 0)))) for s in segs]\n+    total = sum(durs)\n+    return {\n+        \"count\": len(segs),\n+        \"total_duration_s\": round(total, 2),\n+        \"avg_duration_s\": round(total / len(durs), 2),\n+        \"min_duration_s\": round(min(durs), 2),\n+        \"max_duration_s\": round(max(durs), 2),\n+    }\n+\n+\n+def segments_to_json(\n+    segments: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]],\n+    video_id: str,\n+    video_path: Optional[str] = None,\n+    meta: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n+) -\u003e Dict[str, Any]:\n+    \"\"\"Return a JSON-serializable timeline dict.\n+\n+    Shape (stable):\n+    {\n+      \"video_id\": \"...\",\n+      \"video_path\": \"...\",\n+      \"meta\": {...},\n+      \"segments\": [ {\"id\":, \"start_time\":, \"end_time\":, \"duration\":, \"metadata\": {...}}, ... ],\n+      \"stats\": basic_stats(...)\n+    }\n+    \"\"\"\n+    segs = _to_list(segments)\n+    return {\n+        \"video_id\": video_id,\n+        \"video_path\": video_path,\n+        \"meta\": meta or {},\n+        \"segments\": segs,\n+        \"stats\": basic_stats(segs),\n+    }\n+\n+\n+def segments_to_csv(segments: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]], include_header: bool = True) -\u003e str:\n+    \"\"\"Return a CSV string (start,end,duration,shot_count,detector,...) .\n+\n+    Compatible with many editors and the existing golden/slicer schemas\n+    (extra columns are harmless).\n+    \"\"\"\n+    segs = _to_list(segments)\n+    lines: List[str] = []\n+    if include_header:\n+        lines.append(\"start_time,end_time,duration,shot_count,shot_count_confidence,detector,id\")\n+    for s in segs:\n+        md = s.get(\"metadata\") or {}\n+        shot = md.get(\"shot_count\", \"\")\n+        conf = md.get(\"shot_count_confidence\", \"\")\n+        det = md.get(\"detector\", \"\")\n+        lines.append(\n+            f\"{s.get('start_time',0)},{s.get('end_time',0)},{s.get('duration',0)},\"\n+            f\"{shot},{conf},{det},{s.get('id','')}\"\n+        )\n+    return \"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\"\n+\n+\n+def segments_to_edl(segments: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]], title: str = \"Highlights\") -\u003e str:\n+    \"\"\"Return a simple EDL (CMX 3600-ish) for the segments.\n+\n+    Many NLEs will ingest this for a rough cut. Uses record timecode 00:00:00:00\n+    as origin; clips are placed sequentially with the original source in/out.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    segs = _to_list(segments)\n+    lines: List[str] = [f\"TITLE: {title}\", \"FCM: NON-DROP FRAME\"]\n+    for i, s in enumerate(segs, 1):\n+        start = float(s.get(\"start_time\", 0.0))\n+        end = float(s.get(\"end_time\", start))\n+        # EDL is HH:MM:SS:FF (assume 30fps for the timecode display; real edit rate\n+        # is irrelevant for a rough cut — the numbers are just labels here).\n+        def _tc(t: float) -\u003e str:\n+            h = int(t // 3600)\n+            m = int((t % 3600) // 60)\n+            s_ = int(t % 60)\n+            f = int((t - int(t)) * 30)  # 30 fps display\n+            return f\"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s_:02d}:{f:02d}\"\n+        rec_in = _tc((i - 1) * 0.0)  # place back-to-back in the EDL timeline\n+        rec_out = _tc((i - 1) * 0.0 + (end - start))\n+        src_in = _tc(start)\n+        src_out = _tc(end)\n+        lines.append(f\"{i:03d}  AX  V     C        {rec_in} {rec_out} {src_in} {src_out}\")\n+        lines.append(f\"* FROM CLIP NAME: {title} {i}\")\n+        lines.append(\"\")\n+    return \"\\n\".join(lines)"},{"filename":"docs/CODE_MAP.md","status":"modified","additions":1,"changes":1,"patch":"@@ -273,6 +273,7 @@ Entrypoints: `@run_eval.py` (single video score), `@run_phase3_eval.py` (manifes\n | Distill Gemini -\u003e local (thresholds / learned) | `@backend/eval/calibrate_local.py::main` (thresholds) OR `@backend/eval/distill_local.py::main` (learned classifier); **Checked.** 

The conflicts were real (and expected), but we've resolved them.

### Summary of what I found

- **PR #51 (Value Layer / item 3, the one we created last)**: GitHub currently reports `mergeable_state: "clean"`. Local rebase/merge against latest `origin/master` was a no-op ("up to date", no conflicts).

- **PR #50 (reports / Input Quality / item 2)**: This one was the problem — GitHub showed `mergeable_state: "dirty"`. (PR #49 for the detector work had already been merged into master, and our three scaffolding PRs all touched the same progress-tracking sections in the docs.)

### Root cause
Only **one file** had a content conflict:

`docs/LOW_REGRET_MOVES_PLAN.md`

Specifically the top-level sequence list (the "5. Packaging / 6. Detector Runner Abstraction" entries) and the related status/checkpoint notes. 

This is classic when multiple parallel branches + a merge on master (#49) are all editing the same "Done / low-regret status" markdown. The code changes in #50 (the reporting/harvest + spec + test bits) and the new `export.py` + tests in #51 did **not** conflict.

### What I did
1. Reproduced the exact conflict locally on the `feat/extend-reports-input-quality` branch (`git merge origin/master`).
2. Resolved the conflict in `LOW_REGRET_MOVES_PLAN.md` with a clean, consolidated version that:
   - Keeps the short/updated format with PR references.
   - Properly marks items 5/6 (from #49) + the reframed item 8 (#50) + the Value Layer slice (#51).
   - Avoids duplication while preserving the historical detail where it was useful.
3. Cleaned up stray modified files in the working tree that belonged to the #49 changes (not this PR).
4. Committed the merge resolution on the branch.
5. Pushed `d0cf9d4` (the merge commit) to the remote branch for #50.

The push succeeded. GitHub now sees the branch with the latest master merged in + our resolution. The `mergeable_state` came back as "unknown" immediately after the push (normal — GitHub is recalculating); refreshing the PR page in a minute or two should show it as clean / ready to merge (modulo CI).

### Next steps / recommendations
- Refresh https://github.com/avidullu/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/50 — the conflict banner should be gone.
- Do the same quick visual check on #51 (it was already clean).
- If either PR still shows "This branch is out-of-date" or a conflict warning after refresh, we can do another "Update branch" (or I can run `git rebase origin/master` + push on the branch).
- The other files in the PRs (new `export.py`, harvest changes, etc.) should be unaffected.

1→# Backlog — deferred items to revisit
2→
3→Single tracker for work we consciously deferred, so nothing gets lost between sessions. Each item records **what**, **why it's deferred**, and **the trigger to revisit**. Every entry traces to a real decision/PR/doc (no speculative work).
4→

8→_Last updated: 2026-06-07._

11→
12→## Monetization / licensing
13→*(source: `docs/MONETIZATION_AUDIT.md` §7, ADR-005)*
14→
15→- 🟡 **Ultralytics Enterprise pricing** — get a quote **only if** the torchvision/ByteTrack detector (PR #8) proves insufficient on real footage. Currently *not needed* (swap done). Quote-only pricing. **Revisit:** if detector quality forces reconsidering YOLOv8.
16→- 🟡 **Codec patent thresholds (AVC/HEVC/AAC)** — confirm exact Via-LA (AVC) / Access Advance (HEVC) royalty rates & free thresholds **with legal counsel** before relying on the "small SaaS is under threshold" reading. Mitigation already chosen: emit royalty-free AV1/Opus output. **Revisit:** before commercial launch.
17→- 🟡 **Per-checkpoint weight/dataset licenses** — if we borrow pretrained weights (YOLOX, ActionFormer, WASB, TrackNetV3), the *code* license ≠ the distributed *weights/dataset* terms. Either train our own or confirm each checkpoint. **Revisit:** when adopting any borrowed weights.
18→- 🟡 **Real GPU $/video & latency** — benchmark the three LLM-step options (offline ActionFormer vs self-hosted Qwen2.5-VL-7B vs Gemini paid-tier) on the target VM before pricing the product. **Revisit:** when choosing the production LLM step.
19→
20→## Detector swap follow-ups
21→*(source: PR #8, ADR-005)*
22→
23→- 🔴 **`supervision` ByteTrack deprecation** — `sv.ByteTrack` is deprecated as of supervision 0.28.0 and **removed in 0.30.0**; `requirements.txt` is pinned `<0.30.0` as a stopgap. Migrate to `ByteTrackTracker` from the separate `trackers` package before lifting the cap. **Revisit:** before bumping supervision past 0.30.
24→- 🟡 **Real-video validation of the swap** — the torchvision + ByteTrack Stage-1 is unit-tested with mocks but **not yet validated on actual footage** for detection/tracking quality. (ADR-004 separately found player pixel-motion is a weak rally proxy on broadcast video, so expect tuning.) **Revisit:** when a real test clip + the pipeline are run together.
25→- 🟢 **`yolo_hybrid` naming** — the registry key, `yolo_*` config keys, and `yolo-hybrid-*` DB detector tags keep the "yolo" name for back-compat despite no YOLO remaining. Optional rename. **Revisit:** if/when a clean break is worth the migration.
26→
27→## Golden-set subproject

### Refined Pending Priorities (quality / data phase)

**Scaffolding maturation complete** (the three items you green-lit):
- Detector Runner ABC finished (WSL de-risk + common interface + Linux-native extension docs) — #49, merged.
- Input Quality UX extended *inside* the existing per-video reports (audio-present signal + `audio_disabled_or_missing` rule + direct pointers to VIDEO_RECORDING_GUIDELINES) — #50, merged.
- Thin Value Layer slice (pure, additive EDL/JSON/CSV timeline exports + `basic_stats`; 100% covered) — #51, merged.

All done as separate small PRs with the full discipline (miniconda full suite + `--cov`, coverage held at ~64%, docs updated in real time).

**Refined next priorities (in rough current order for the quality-improvement phase):**

1. **Golden set expansion + data flywheel UX** (highest priority once the new videos land)  
   Ingest the mid-next-week batch, expand calibration/cross-val/regression baselines, make label contribution + safe retraining easy and non-contaminating (human correction that feeds golden, clear eval vs. training separation). This is the engine for the self-improving vision.

2. **Hybrid unification + observability hooks** (deferred item 7, now higher)  
   Extract shared logic from the two (or three) hybrids before we wire richer AI timing/metrics into the reports. One clean place for timing, detector health, candidate quality signals, etc.

3. **Detector / windowing / candidate quality iteration (with real new data)**  
   Re-tune velocity/merge/min-duration + rally gate on the expanded golden set. Validate current substrates on actual footage. Light audio fusion experiments (building on the E1 probe + `hit_detector`). Use the reports to surface the next accuracy bottlenecks.

4. **Deeper Value Layer (beyond the minimal exports slice)**  
   Per-rally tagging/notes that write back to `candidate_segments` (and can feed golden), basic per-player/match stats, clip gallery/scrubbing helpers, human correction loop.

5. **Input quality enforcement & guidance polish**  
   Turn more of the VIDEO_RECORDING_GUIDELINES dimensions into stronger validators or report rules/footguns. Better "your video is good enough / here's exactly what to fix" UX via the customer verdict.

6. **Multi-sport maturation + recording best practices**  
   Sport profiles, audio profiles, motion priors, annotation taxonomies + stronger in-product capture quality guidance (leveraging the reports surface we just extended).

7. **SaaS / multi-user / async foundations** (if the hosted path becomes primary)  
   Async jobs, progress, storage lifecycle, auth/tenancy primitives. (Lower until the monetization model clarifies.)

8. **Polish & distribution**  
   Installer/first-run story, example assets, codec/output decisions, SaaS economics.

**Recommendation for the immediate next 1–2 weeks (new videos arriving):**  
Put ~80% of the energy on #1 (data flywheel + golden expansion) and #3 (quality iteration against the fresh corpus). Use the new data to drive measurable gains via the eval harness + regression gates + reports. Only after that do we need unification (#2) and the deeper value work (#4).

The reports layer + the minimal Value Layer exports we just shipped give us a perfect place to surface new quality signals and footguns as we tune.

### 177. tool_result

1→# Badminton Highlight & Rally Indexer
2→
3→A local, AI-powered web application and CLI utility designed to process long-form badminton recordings (e.g., from GoPro), run comprehensive pluggable validations (quality, integrity, and relevance checks), index all rallies into a relational SQLite database, and dynamically compile custom highlight reels using high-speed FFmpeg stitching.
4→
5→The pipeline is built around three pluggable registries — **validators**, **segmenters**, and **AI providers** — plus a **sport profile** registry so the same engine can target other racket/court sports in the future. Badminton is the default profile.
6→
7→> **Where this is heading:** the project is evolving from an AI-API-dependent prototype into a self-improving tool that learns rally detection *offline* from a commercially-licensed, annotated corpus. See **[docs/ROADMAP.md](docs/ROADMAP.md)** for the four-phase plan and **[docs/DECISIONS.md](docs/DECISIONS.md)** (ADR-002…004) for the rationale.
8→
9→---
10→
11→## 🚀 Key Features
12→
13→*   **Pluggable Video Validation Framework**: Easily extendable base-class architecture that automatically runs checks on:
14→    *   *Static Format & Resolution*: Verifies container is MP4, resolution >= 720p, and framerate >= 30 FPS.
15→    *   *PTS Timeline Continuity*: Scans keyframe timestamps for timeline gaps, jumps, or editing splices to detect tampering or splices.
16→    *   *Scene Cut Density Check*: Measures sub-sampled HSV frame histogram correlation to verify visual cuts. A continuous raw camera stream should have near 0 cuts.
17→    *   *Blurriness Check*: Calculates average frame Laplacian variance to reject out-of-focus footage.
18→    *   *Relevance (Is it Badminton?)*: Uses high-performance HSV color court matching to identify court layouts.
19→*   **Three Pluggable Rally Segmenters** (`backend/indexer/`):
20→    *   `yolo_hybrid` **(default)** — two-stage pipeline. Stage 1 runs **torchvision person detection** (Faster R-CNN, BSD-licensed) with **ByteTrack** tracking (supervision, MIT-licensed) to cheaply filter the video down to a small set of high-motion candidate windows. Stage 2 hands each candidate (with a small padding) to the AI provider for high-precision semantic boundaries and shot counts. Massively cheaper than running the LLM on the full video. *(The registry key remains `yolo_hybrid` for back-compat; the AGPL-licensed YOLOv8 was replaced with permissive components — see [docs/MONETIZATION_AUDIT.md](docs/MONETIZATION_AUDIT.md) and ADR-005.)*

Code health is good for a research-to-product effort but the flat `backend/` layout and several other issues need addressing for commercial readiness.

### 193. assistant

**Proposal: `docs/` declutter**

### Current situation (as of now)
`docs/` contains 17 files that mix three categories:

**Live / high-signal (for ramp-up + constant updates)**
- `BACKLOG.md`, `CODE_MAP.md`, `COMMERCIAL_READINESS_REVIEW.md`, `ROADMAP.md`
- `VIDEO_RECORDING_GUIDELINES.md`, `HOW_RALLY_DETECTION_WORKS.md`, `EVALUATION.md`
- `TRACKNET_WSL_SETUP.md`
- The three `GOLDEN_SET_*` files (still very active for the data flywheel phase)

**Historical / completed / one-off**
- `LOW_REGRET_MOVES_PLAN.md` — all low-regret items + the three scaffolding items (#49, #50, #51) are now merged and done. Perfect candidate for archiving (as you suggested).
- `DECISIONS.md` (ADRs)
- `MONETIZATION_AUDIT.md`

**Research / superseded plans**
- `AUDIO_RALLY_DETECTION_PLAN.md`
- `RALLY_DETECTION_RESEARCH.md`
- `AUDIO_E1_FINDINGS.md`

This mix makes the directory feel heavier than it needs to be for someone trying to ramp up or find "what's current."

### Goals for the declutter
- **Live docs at the top level** — the files a new person (or returning contributor) should read first for architecture, current priorities, guidelines, and active projects.
- Historical/completed/one-time material goes into `archive/` (still fully searchable via git, just not in the way).
- Add lightweight `README.md` files so the intent is obvious.
- Keep changes minimal and reversible.

### Proposed structure

```text
docs/
├── README.md                          # NEW: "Live docs live here. Historical → archive/"
├── BACKLOG.md
├── CODE_MAP.md
├── COMMERCIAL_READINESS_REVIEW.md
├── EVALUATION.md
├── GOLDEN_SET_IMPLEMENTATION.md
├── GOLDEN_SET_PHASE1_VIDEOS.md
├── GOLDEN_SET_VENDORING.md
├── HOW_RALLY_DETECTION_WORKS.md
├── ROADMAP.md
├── TRACKNET_WSL_SETUP.md
├── VIDEO_RECORDING_GUIDELINES.md
└── archive/
    ├── README.md                      # NEW: policy + index
    ├── low-regret-moves/
    │   └── LOW_REGRET_MOVES_PLAN.md   # (with big HISTORICAL header + link back to current REVIEW)
    ├── decisions/
    │   └── DECISIONS.md
    ├── research/
    │   ├── AUDIO_E1_FINDINGS.md
    │   ├── AUDIO_RALLY_DETECTION_PLAN.md
    │   └── RALLY_DETECTION_RESEARCH.md
    └── audits/
        └── MONETIZATION_AUDIT.md
```

**Result**: Top-level `docs/` drops from 17 files to ~12 focused live ones.

### Specific recommendations

1. **`LOW_REGRET_MOVES_PLAN.md`**  
   Move to `archive/low-regret-moves/`. Add a prominent header at the top:
   > **HISTORICAL / COMPLETED** — All items (low-regret 1-6 + scaffolding #49/#50/#51) merged June 2026. Current state is in `COMMERCIAL_READINESS_REVIEW.md`.

2. **DECISIONS.md**  
   Move to `archive/decisions/`. Most code references to specific ADRs (ADR-001 etc.) can stay as-is (they're historical by nature). We only update the main mentions in `README.md` and a couple of docs.

3. **Research & audit files**  
   Grouped logically under `archive/research/` and `archive/audits/`.

4. **New guardrails**
   - `docs/README.md` — short "start here" + points to archive for old stuff.
   - `docs/archive/README.md` — clear policy so future contributors know the convention.

### Cross-reference updates needed (minimal)
- `COMMERCIAL_READINESS_REVIEW.md` (the pointer to LOW_REGRET)
- `README.md` (project structure diagram + a couple of links)
- A few internal doc links (already done in the branch below)

Code comments that say `docs/DECISIONS.md` (ADR-001 etc.) can be left alone or batch-updated later — they still resolve via git.

- `BACKLOG.md`, `CODE_MAP.md`, `COMMERCIAL_READINESS_REVIEW.md`, `EVALUATION.md`, `GOLDEN_SET_*` (3), `HOW_RALLY_DETECTION_WORKS.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, `TRACKNET_WSL_SETUP.md`, `VIDEO_RECORDING_GUIDELINES.md` + the new `README.md`

`archive/` has the policy file + the six moved documents (with the historical header on the low-regret plan).

=== projects/README.md (the rule) ===
# projects/

This directory holds **completed project phases, major initiatives, and historical checkpoints**.

## Archival Rule (strict)
A document or directory may only be placed here when its work is in a **terminal state**:
- **DONE**
- **COMPLETED**
- **REJECTED**

**Never** archive anything that is in-flight, planned, or still pending active work.

When archiving:
1. Add (or ensure) a clear status header at the top of the main document, e.g.:
   ```
   **STATUS: COMPLETED** (merged June 2026, PRs #49, #50, #51)
   ```
2. Link back to the current living documents (`../COMMERCIAL_READINESS_REVIEW.md`, `../BACKLOG.md`, etc.).
3. For multi-doc phases, create a dated or named subdirectory (see `checkpoints/` and `quality-iterations/` below).

## Organization
- `low-regret-moves/` — The 2026 low-regret + scaffolding phase (now complete).
- `decisions/` — Historical ADRs and major architectural decisions (when the active discussion has concluded).
- `research/` — Completed or superseded research notes and plans.
- `checkpoints/` — Snapshots of major project state for regression detection (see below).
- `quality-iterations/` — Future home for completed quality improvement cycles (e.g. `2026-06-first-labeled-batch/`).

## Checkpoints for Regression Detection
We keep dated snapshots here so we can detect regressions in project health, priorities, or quality baselines over time.


=== Example STATUS in archived doc ===
# Low-Regret Moves Plan — Config Model First

**STATUS: COMPLETED** (all items merged June 2026 — PRs #49, #50, #51)

> Historical record of the low-regret + scaffolding maturation phase.
> Current state and next priorities live in [../../COMMERCIAL_READINESS_REVIEW.md](../../COMMERCIAL_READINESS_REVIEW.md)
> and [../../BACKLOG.md](../../BACKLOG.md).

**Date:** 2026-06-07 (final update)  
**Owner / Driver:** Grok (second-in-command) + avidullu + Claude

---

=== Example checkpoint ===
# Checkpoint: 2026-06 Scaffolding Complete

**Date:** June 2026  
**STATUS: COMPLETED**  
**Trigger:** User merged the final scaffolding PRs (#49 DetectorRunner finish, #50 Reports-based Input Quality, #51 minimal Value Layer).

## Purpose
This checkpoint captures the state of the project once the core scaffolding (typed config, layered structure, Result contract, logging/packaging, DetectorRunner abstraction, observability reports as the quality surface, and first value-layer exports) was mature enough to iterate on.

It provides a regression-detection baseline for future quality work.

## Key Documents at Checkpoint Time
- COMMERCIAL_READINESS_REVIEW.md (the refined post-merge version)
- LOW_REGRET_MOVES_PLAN.md (final completed status)
- Related updates to CODE_MAP.md, BACKLOG.md, etc.

See git history around the merges of #49/#50/#51 for the exact snapshots.

## What Comes Next (per user)
- New labeled videos arriving mid-next week.
